   Periodically random block shifts to escape.

- For each step:

  If M_c <= target? choose weights = softmax((c-M_c)/tau_g) or maybe top peaks.

Now, will LP-like g improve? Let's reason: Given a baseline, we solve for g with sum1 minimizing max a*g. Since g can be quite different. For a decreasing inverse sqrt, maybe optimal g is increasing? Let's see: a_i large near0. To make a*g central small, pair large a_i with small g_{k-i}. If g is increasing (small near0, large near n), central at k=n-1 pairs a_i with g_{n-1-i}: large a_i with small g for i small? If g increasing, g_{n-1-i} near n-i: for i small, g large? Wait increasing: g_0 small, g_{n-1} large. Pair i small a large with g_{n-1-i} large -> bad. To minimize central, want g decreasing (large where a reversed small? central pairs a_i with g_{n-1-i}; if a decreasing, g increasing gives large a with large g? Actually a decreasing: a_0 large, a_{n-1} small. g increasing: g_{n-1} large; pair at i=0: a_0*g_{n-1} large; bad. g decreasing: g_0 large; pair i=0: a_0*g_{n-1} small; pair i large: a small*g0 large; central sum moderate. So g likely decreasing similarly? If g=a, central max. Maybe g with even steeper decrease? To reduce central, shift g mass to left? But then right tail peaks? The LP for all k may produce a g that is mirror? The update may oscillate.

If g decreases more, blending makes a steeper, maybe low central but right endpoint? Could improve.

Could the target sequence be a* times a*r with a specific decreasing shape? We can trust optimizer.

def normalize_nonneg(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<=1e-300:
        x=np.ones_like(x)/x.size
    else:
        x=x/s
    return x
```

```

def mul_step(a, d, base_etas, L, cur_sc):
    scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if scale<=1e-300: return None,None
    best=None; best_sc=cur_sc

       logit=-eta*d
       logit=np.clip(logit,-12,12)
       b=a*np.exp(logit)

       if sc<best_sc:

    return best,best_sc
```

Potential issue: `normx` always normalizes sum to1; parent values maybe sum huge but ok. Score exact with sum1.

`score(a,L)` returns `2*n*max(np.max(conv)) / (sum(a)**2)`. Since normx sum1, but use general.

`pweights(c,r)`: If cmax<=0 return zeros; if r==0 uniform? We can implement using normalized u,cmax. For r can be large, u^r underflows for small u; okay. If sum<=0, fallback one max.
```
def pweights(c,r):
  cmax=float(np.max(c))
  if cmax<=0: w=zeros; w[np.argmax(c)]=1; return w
  u=c/cmax
  if r<=0: w=np.ones_like(c)/c.size; return w
  w=np.power(u,r)
  s=w.sum()
  if not finite or s<=0:
     idx=c>=cmax-1e-12*cmax; w=idx.astype; s=w.sum(); if s==0: w=zeros, argmax1
  return w/s
```
If r=32, for cmax maybe positive; underflow ok.

`wgrad(var,w,L)`: Ensure L is L2. If L not large enough for w length+var length-1, use next? We pass L2.
```
def wgrad(var,w,L):
   n=var.size
   rv=var[::-1]
   out=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(rv,L)*np.fft.rfft(w,L), L)
   out=out[n-1:2*n-1]
   out=np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
   return out
```

`conv_self`/`conv_cross` could use `np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)`; after irfft, length L, take first 2n-1. For cross maybe same.
```
def conv_self(a,L):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=L)
    c=c[:2*a.size-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
    return c
```
Maybe use `fa*fa` not `fa*fa.conj()` because convolution with self not autocorr. Correct. For cross a,g: `rfft(a)*rfft(g)`.

```

def mul_candidates(a,d,etas,L,cur):

Random perturb helper:
```
def perturb(a,rng,amp):
   n=a.size
   x=np.arange(n)
   smooth = amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*(rng.random()*x + rng.random()))
   # better: low-freq random: coefficients for first 5 cos
   d=rng.normal(0,amp, n)
   cand=a*np.exp(d)
   return normx(cand)
```
But random full noise may hurt; do occasional if stagnant. Use small amp 0.05-0.2.

Initial generation: Keep list small. Need not include too many to save time but diverse. Generate algorithmically:
```
def make_inits(n,parent):
   res=[]
   if parent valid: res.append(parent)
   idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   x=idx/(n if n>1 else 1)
   base=np.power(idx+0.5,-0.5)
   res.append(base)
   # parent transformations
   for beta in (-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0):
       res.append(base*np.exp(beta*x))
   # power
   for p in (-0.5,-0.4,-0.3,-0.2,-0.1,0.0,0.2):
       res.append(np.power(idx+0.5,p))
   # linear inverse with right bias
   for c in (0.2,0.5,1.0):
       res.append(base*np.exp(c*x)) # duplicate
   # quadratic
   for g in (-0.5,0.5):
       res.append(base*np.exp(-0.5*(2*x-1)**2 + g*x)) # no long
   # damp first region
   m=max(4,n//2000)

Now, let's reason if `make_g` could produce g that is too similar to a; if it starts from a and update gradient of cross constraints, since at g=a, c=b. The weights pweights(c) emphasize peaks. grad_i = a_i * h_i where h_i=∑w_k a_{k-i}. Update g_i *= exp(-eta (grad_i - mean)). This reduces g at positions contributing to peaks, relative. It is basically the same mirror descent as a gradient but with variable g. Since g starts a, after few steps g differs. Blending a with g might be similar to direct gradient but perhaps better because g constraint solves all cross peaks. Good.

Clean/normalize:
```
def normalize_pos(a):
    x=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    # floor
    x+=1e-18
    s=float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       x=np.ones_like(x); s=float(x.size)
    return x/s
```
Adding floor before sum changes sum; okay. If all zero, becomes n*1e-18, sum 5e-15 >1e-300; normalized to uniform. Good.

def try_add(list,n,arr):
    arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size!=n: return
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    if float(np.sum(np.maximum(arr,0.0)))>1e-300:
        list.append(arr)

LP `build_g`:
```
def build_g(a,g,L,L2,steps,deadline):
    g=normalize_pos(g)
    for _ in range(steps):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       c=conv_cross(a,g,L)
       w=power_weights(c,6.0)
       h=weighted_var(g,w,L2)
       grad=a*h
       mu=float(np.dot(g,grad))
       d=grad-mu
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       if scale <= 1e-300: break
       logit=-np.clip(d/(scale+1e-300), -2.5, 2.5)
       g=normalize_pos(g*np.exp(logit))
    return g
```
Using d/scale means eta=1. If d large, relative step maybe 2.5. Could be okay. Maybe if no improvement, smaller? Could use clip ±1.5.

`multiply_step` uses full score for each eta. `score_of` uses sum; for normalized candidates sum ~1. Could optimize by precomputing n and assuming sum1 but fine.

`random_perturb`:
```
def random_perturb(a,rng,amp):
    # smooth random via random low-frequency sine
    ph1=rng.random()*6.283185307179586
    ph2=rng.random()*6.283185307179586
    idx=np.arange(a.size)
    d=amp*(np.sin(2.0*np.pi*idx/ max(1,a.size) + ph1) + np.sin(4.0*np.pi*idx/ max(1,a.size)+ph2))
    cand=a*np.exp(d)
    return normalize_pos(cand)
```
Full random noise maybe too rough; smooth good. Constants pi okay.

def normalize_pos(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size==0: x=np.ones(1)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)+1e-18
    s=x.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
        x=np.ones_like(x); s=float(x.size)
    return x/s
```

```
def conv_self(a,L):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=L)
    c=c[:2*a.size-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
    return c
```
`conv_cross` similar.

`score_of`:
```
def score_of(a,L):
    c=conv_self(a,L)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or s<=1e-300: return (np.inf,c,M)
    return (float(2.0*a.size*M/(s*s)), c, M)
```
Since we often only need scalar, but sometimes c. For line search need only scalar; could have `score_val`:

def score_val(a,L):
    c=conv_self(a,L); M=...; return scalar
```

`power_weights`:
```
def power_weights(c,r):
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    m=c.size
    if cmax<=0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        w=np.zeros(m); im=int(np.argmax(c)); w[im]=1; return w
    u=c/cmax
    if r<=0:
        return np.full(m,1.0/float(m))
    w=np.power(u,r)
    s=float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
        mask=c>=cmax-1e-12*max(1.0,cmax)
        w=mask.astype(np.float64)
        s=float(np.sum(w))
        if s<=0: w=np.zeros(m); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; s=1
    return w/s
```

`weighted_var` as above.

`mul_step` maybe use `score_val` faster? Implement `score_val` separately.

def score_val(a,L):
    c=conv_self(a,L)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or s<=1e-300: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*a.size*M/(s*s))
```
Then no tuple. Initial can use `score_val`. But we need b in main; use conv_self.

def add_v(lst,n,v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size!=n: return
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0)
    if float(np.sum(np.maximum(v,0.0)))>1e-300:
        lst.append(v)
```

```
def make_initials(n,parent):
    out=[]
    if parent is not None: add_v(out,n,parent)
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    x=idx/float(n) if n>1 else np.zeros(n)
    base=np.power(idx+0.5,-0.5)
    add_v(out,n,base)
    add_v(out,n,np.ones(n))
    for p in (-0.45,-0.35,-0.25,-0.15,-0.05,0.05,0.15):
        add_v(out,n,np.power(idx+0.5,p))
    for b in (-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0):
        add_v(out,n,base*np.exp(b*x))
    for d in (0.2,0.5,0.75):
        add_v(out,n,base*(1.0-d+d*x))
        add_v(out,n,base*(1.0-d*(1.0-x)))  # lowers right? if want
    m=max(4,n//500)
    for fac in (0.05,0.2,0.5):
        v=base.copy(); v[:m]=v[:m]*fac; add_v(out,n,v)
    return out
```

def build_g(a,g,L,L2,steps,deadline,wex=4.0):
   g=normalize_pos(g)
   for _ in range(steps):
     if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
     c=conv_cross(a,g,L)
     w=power_weights(c,wex)

     grad=a*h

     d=grad-mu
     scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
     if scale<=1e-300: break

     g=normalize_pos(g*np.exp(logit))

```

           final_sc=sc_eff; final_best=out
else:
   ... use FFT
return [float(v) for v in final_best]
```

    out=[]

    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def pw2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_pos(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        x = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0) + 1e-18
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        x = np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(x.size)
    return x / s


def conv_self(a, L):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=L)
    c = c[:2 * a.size - 1]
    return np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)


def conv_cross(a, b, L):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, n=L)
    c = c[:2 * a.size - 1]
    return np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)


def score_val(a, L):
    c = conv_self(a, L)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0 or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * M / (s * s))


def power_weights(c, r):
    m = c.size
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    if r <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.clip(c / cmax, 0.0, 1.0)
    w = np.power(u, r)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        mask = c >= cmax - 1e-12 * max(1.0, cmax)
        w = mask.astype(np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            s = 1.0
    return w / s


def weighted_var(var, w, L):
    n = var.size
    rv = var[::-1]
    out = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(rv, n=L) * np.fft.rfft(w, n=L), n=L)
    out = out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)


def mul_step(a, d, etas, L, cur_sc):
    if d.size == 0:
        return None, cur_sc
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
    if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-300:
        return None, cur_sc
    best = None
    best_sc = cur_sc
    for e in etas:
        logit = np.clip(-float(e) * d / rms, -12.0, 12.0)
        cand = normalize_pos(a * np.exp(logit))
        sc = score_val(cand, L)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = cand
    return best, best_sc


def build_g(a, g, L, L2, steps, deadline, wex):
    g = normalize_pos(g)
    for _ in range(max(0, int(steps))):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        c = conv_cross(a, g, L)
        w = power_weights(c, float(wex))
        h = weighted_var(g, w, L2)
        grad = a * h
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        mu = float(np.dot(g, grad))
        d = grad - mu
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(d * d)))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-300:
            break
        logit = np.clip(-d / rms, -2.0, 2.0)
        g = normalize_pos(g * np.exp(logit))
    return g


def random_perturb(a, rng, amp):
    n = a.size
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    den = float(max(1, n))
    p1 = float(rng.random() * 6.283185307179586)
    p2 = float(rng.random() * 6.283185307179586)
    d = float(amp) * (
        np.sin(6.283185307179586 * idx / den + p1) +
        np.sin(12.566370614359172 * idx / den + p2)
    )
    return normalize_pos(a * np.exp(d))


def add_v(out, n, v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size != n:
        return
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(np.maximum(v, 0.0))) > 1e-300:
        out.append(v)


def make_initials(n, parent):
    out = []
    if parent is not None:
        add_v(out, n, parent)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = idx / float(n) if n > 1 else np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = np.power(idx + 0.5, -0.5)
    add_v(out, n, base)
    add_v(out, n, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for al in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 32.0):
        add_v(out, n, np.power(idx + al, -0.5))

    for g in (0.001, 0.005, 0.02, 0.08):
        add_v(out, n, np.power(idx + g * float(n), -0.5))

    for p in (-0.4, -0.3, -0.2, -0.1, -0.05, 0.05, 0.1):
        add_v(out, n, np.power(idx + 0.5, p))

    for b in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        add_v(out, n, base * np.exp(b * x))

    for d in (0.2, 0.5, 0.75):
        add_v(out, n, base * (1.0 - d + d * x))

    m = max(4, n // 200)
    mm = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    for c in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        v = base.copy()
        v[:m] = v[:m] * np.exp(-c * mm)
        add_v(out, n, v)

    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim != 1:
            parent = parent.ravel()
        parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
        if parent.size == 0:
            parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n = 256
    else:
        n = int(parent.size)
    if n < 1:
        n = 1
        parent = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L = pw2(2 * n - 1)
    L2 = pw2(3 * n - 2)

    start = time.monotonic()
    hard = start + max(20.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    deadline = hard - 60.0
    if deadline < start + 1.0:
        deadline = start + 1.0

    inits = make_initials(n, parent)
    best_a = normalize_pos(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    best_sc = score_val(best_a, L)

    for arr in inits:
        a = normalize_pos(arr)
        sc = score_val(a, L)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a

    a = best_a.copy()
    sc = best_sc
    g = a.copy()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stag = 0
    it = 0
    target = 1.50274349
    blend_ts = (0.05, 0.15, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = conv_self(a, L)
        M = float(np.max(b))
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        sc = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s)) if M > 0.0 and s > 1e-300 else np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            stag = 0
        else:
            stag += 1

        if best_sc <= target + 1e-12:
            break

        updated = False
        if it % 3 == 0 or stag > 25:
            rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
            steps = 5 if rem > 300 else (3 if rem > 100 else 1)
            wex = 2.0 if stag < 12 else (6.0 if stag < 70 else 16.0)
            if steps > 0:
                g = build_g(a, g, L, L2, steps, deadline, wex)
                gsc = score_val(g, L)
                if np.isfinite(gsc):
                    if gsc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = gsc
                        best_a = g.copy()
                    if gsc < sc:
                        a = g.copy()
                        sc = gsc
                        stag = 0
                        updated = True
                for t in blend_ts:
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                        break
                    cand = normalize_pos((1.0 - t) * a + t * g)
                    sc2 = score_val(cand, L)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc2
                        best_a = cand.copy()
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc:
                        a = cand
                        sc = sc2
                        stag = 0
                        updated = True
                        break

        if updated:
            it += 1
            continue

        if time.monotonic() < deadline:
            p = 1.0 if stag < 5 else (2.0 if stag < 25 else (6.0 if stag < 90 else 20.0))
            w = power_weights(b, p)
            h = weighted_var(a, w, L2)
            d = h - float(np.dot(a, h))
            d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
            cand, sc2 = mul_step(a, d, (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0), L, sc)
            if cand is not None:
                a = cand
                sc = sc2
                stag = 0
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    if best_sc <= target + 1e-12:
                        break

            if time.monotonic() < deadline and it % 3 == 0:
                dp = a * d
                cand, sc2 = mul_step(a, dp, (0.05, 0.2, 0.8), L, sc)
                if cand is not None:
                    a = cand
                    sc = sc2
                    stag = 0
                    if sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        if best_sc <= target + 1e-12:
                            break

        if stag > 40 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            for amp in (0.03, 0.08, 0.2):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                cand = random_perturb(a, rng, amp)
                sc2 = score_val(cand, L)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc:
                    a = cand
                    sc = sc2
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = cand.copy()
                    if best_sc <= target + 1e-12:
                        break
            stag = 0

        it += 1

    final_best = best_a.copy()
    final_sc = best_sc
    final_cands = [best_a, a]
    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
        final_cands.append(parent)
    for arr in inits[:4]:
        final_cands.append(arr)

    use_exact = (n <= 20000)
    for arr in final_cands:
        if arr is None:
            continue
        out = normalize_pos(arr)
        if out.size != n:
            continue
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        sc_eff = score_val(out, L)
        if use_exact and time.monotonic() < hard - 5.0:
            try:
                sc_ex = float(evaluate_sequence(out.tolist()))
                if np.isfinite(sc_ex):
                    sc_eff = sc_ex
            except Exception:
                pass
        if np.isfinite(sc_eff) and sc_eff < final_sc:
            final_sc = sc_eff
            final_best = out

    if final_best is None or final_best.size == 0:
        final_best = normalize_pos(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    final_best = np.clip(final_best, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(final_best)) < 0.01:
        final_best = normalize_pos(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    return [float(v) for v in final_best]
```
